  function getQueryParams(){
    var search = window.location.search;
    var params = new URLSearchParams(search);
    var paramObj = {};
    for(var value of params.keys()) {
         paramObj[value] = params.get(value);
     }
    return paramObj;
  }

<script type="text/javascript">
  		$(document).ready(function(){
  // Add smooth scrolling to all links
  $("a").on('click', function(event) {

    // Make sure this.hash has a value before overriding default behavior
    if (this.hash !== "") {
      // Prevent default anchor click behavior
      event.preventDefault();

      // Store hash
      var hash = this.hash;

      // Using jQuery's animate() method to add smooth page scroll
      // The optional number (800) specifies the number of milliseconds it takes to scroll to the specified area
      $('html, body').animate({
        scrollTop: $(hash).offset().top
      }, 800, function(){

        // Add hash (#) to URL when done scrolling (default click behavior)
        window.location.hash = hash;
      });
    } // End if
  });
});
  </script>

        function setCookie(cname, cvalue, exdays) {
            var d = new Date();
            d.setTime(d.getTime() + (exdays*24*60*60*1000));
            var expires = "expires="+ d.toUTCString();
            document.cookie = cname + "=" + cvalue + ";" + expires + ";path=/";
        }


        var onSuccess = function(location){
          console.log("GEO IP received: ", location);
          var Currencies = window.customData["Currencies"];
          window.customData["location"] = location; 
          window.customData["currency"] = Currencies[location["country"]["iso_code"]]["currency_code"];
          
		  window["optimizely"].push({
            type: "user",
            attributes: {
              country: customData["location"]["country"]["iso_code"]
            }
          });
    	
    	  try{
    	    var country = customData["location"]["country"]["iso_code"];
          } catch(e){
           country = "US" 
          }
            
    
          if( ["US", "GB", "AU", "NZ", "CA", "FR", "AE", "DE", "IE"].indexOf(country) > -1){
			window['optimizely'] = window['optimizely'] || [];
            window['optimizely'].push({
              type: "page",
              pageName: "anglo_free_trial"
            });
          } else {
            window['optimizely'] = window['optimizely'] || [];
            window['optimizely'].push({
              type: "page",
              pageName: "international_free_trial_test"
            });
            
          }

		  $("head").trigger("geoReady", Currencies[location["country"]["iso_code"]]);
        };

        var onError = function(error){
          console.log("Geo IP error: ", error);
        };

        window.geoip2 && window.geoip2.country(onSuccess, onError);
      })();

<script>
  updateCartIconCounter();
  
  $('.shop-dropdown').click(function(evt){
    evt.preventDefault();
    $('.header-dropdown-menu').slideToggle('400', function(){
      if (typeof changeHeaderBackground !== 'undefined') {
        if($(".header-dropdown-menu").is(":visible")){
          $('.sample-header .site-header__mobile-nav').css('background-color', '#F5F5F4');
        } else{
          $('.sample-header .site-header__mobile-nav').css('background-color', 'transparent');
        }
      }
    });
  })
  
  function updateCartIconCounter () {
    total = 0;
    if (localStorage.getItem("cartProducts") !== null) {
      cartProducts = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("cartProducts"));
      total = cartProducts.length;
    }

    $('.cart___counter').html(total);
  }
</script>

<script>
  $('.skincare-concern-option .checkbox').click(function(evt){
    evt.preventDefault();
    
    if ($(this).hasClass('active')){
      $(this).removeClass('active');
    } else if ($('.skincare-concern-option .checkbox.active').length < 2) {
      $(this).addClass('active');
    }
  })
  
  $('.get-started-step-1-button').click(function(evt){
    evt.preventDefault();
    
    window.trackingData = {
      url: window.location.href,
    }
    amplitude.getInstance().logEvent("HP: Start Duo Flow", trackingData);
    
    amplitude.getInstance().logEvent("HP: Selected Skin Concerns Section Opened", trackingData);
    
    $('.get-started-step-1').hide();
    $('.get-started-step-3').hide();
    $('.get-started-step-4').hide();
    $('.get-started-step-2').css('display', 'flex');
  });

<script>
  $('.cart-recommendation-purchase-type').click(function(evt) {
    evt.preventDefault();
    
    $('.cart-recommendation-purchase-type').removeClass('active');
    $(this).addClass('active');
    
    var recommendedProductIds = [];
    $(".cart-recommendation-section").find(".cart-recommendation-product").each(function(){ recommendedProductIds.push(this.id.substring(8)); });
    
    allPossibleIds = Object.values(getStartedProductsOptions);
    
    allPossibleIds = allPossibleIds.filter( ( el ) => !recommendedProductIds.includes( el ) );
    
    $('.recommended-products').empty();
    
    allPossibleIds.splice(0,2).forEach(function(item) {
      $('.recommended-products').append( renderSuggestedProduct(item) );
    });
    
    getRecommendedCartTotal();
  });

  function renderRecommendedProduct (product_id) {
    productAttributes = allProducts['Individual Items'].filter(function(item) {if(item.id == product_id){return item}});
    
    return `
      <div id="product-${productAttributes[0].id}" class="cart-recommendation-product">
        <div class="cart-recommendation-product-image">
          <img src="${productAttributes[0].image_url}" alt="" />
        </div>
        <div class="cart-recommendation-product-margin"></div>
        <div class="cart-recommendation-product-title">
          <h5>${productAttributes[0].title}</h5>
        </div>
        <div class="cart-recommendation-product-removal"><h5 onclick="removeRecommendedProduct(this); return false;" class="remove-cart-recommendation-product">-</h5></div>
      </div>
    `;
  }
  
  function getRecommendedCartTotal() {
    var discountedPrice = 0;
    var actualPrice = 0;
    var discountedAmount = 0;
    
    defaultRecommendedCartPrice = 'sub_price';
    if ($('.cart-recommendation-purchase-type.active').length > 0) {
      purchaseTypeId = $('.cart-recommendation-purchase-type.active')[0].id;
      
      if (purchaseTypeId === 'cart-recommendation-one-time-purchase') {
        defaultRecommendedCartPrice = 'price';
        $('.isSubscription').hide();
      } else if (purchaseTypeId === 'cart-recommendation-subscription-purchase') {
        defaultRecommendedCartPrice = 'sub_price';
        $('.isSubscription').show();
      }
    }
    
    $('.cart-recommendation-product').each(function(){
      productId = this.id.substring(8);
      productAttributes = allProducts['Individual Items'].filter(function(item) {if(item.id == productId){return item}});
      productSubPrice = parseFloat(productAttributes[0][defaultRecommendedCartPrice].toString().replace(/[^0-9-.]/g, ''));
      
      productPrice = parseFloat(productAttributes[0].price.toString().replace(/[^0-9-.]/g, ''));
      
      
      discountedPrice += parseFloat(productSubPrice);
      actualPrice += parseFloat(productPrice);
      discountedAmount = parseFloat(actualPrice) - parseFloat(discountedPrice);
    });
    
    $('.discounted-cart-price').html( formatPrice(allProducts.Accessories[0].price, parseFloat(discountedPrice)) );
    $('.difference-cart-price').html( formatPrice(allProducts.Accessories[0].price, parseFloat(discountedAmount).toFixed(2)) );
  }
  
  
  function removeRecommendedProduct (div) {
    $(div).closest('.cart-recommendation-product').remove();
    
    var recommendedProductIds = [];
    $(".cart-recommendation-section").find(".cart-recommendation-product").each(function(){ recommendedProductIds.push(this.id.substring(8)); });
    
    allPossibleIds = Object.values(getStartedProductsOptions);
    
    allPossibleIds = allPossibleIds.filter( ( el ) => !recommendedProductIds.includes( el ) );
    
    $('.recommended-products').empty();
    
    allPossibleIds.splice(0,2).forEach(function(item) {
      $('.recommended-products').append( renderSuggestedProduct(item) );
    });
    
    getRecommendedCartTotal();
  }
  
  $('.recommendCartCurrencySelect').change(function(evt) {
    evt.preventDefault();
    
    // Get all the Ids for recommended section and suggested section 
    recommendedProductIds = $(".cart-recommendation-product").map(function(){return this.id.substring(8)}).toArray();
    suggestedProductIds = $(".recommended-product").map(function(){return this.id.substring(8)}).toArray();
    
    // Clear recommended section and suggested section
    $('.recommended-products').empty();
    $(".cart-recommendation-section").empty();
    
    // Use currency to get all products with new currency
    var recommendCartCurrency = $(".recommendCartCurrencySelect").val();
    allProducts = convertCurrencyToProduct(allCurrencies[recommendCartCurrency]);
    
    // Use Ids to fill recommended section and suggested section
    recommendedProductIds.forEach(function(item) {
      $('.cart-recommendation-section').append( renderRecommendedProduct(item) );
    });
    
    suggestedProductIds.forEach(function(item) {
      $('.recommended-products').append( renderSuggestedProduct(item) );
    });
    
    getRecommendedCartTotal();
  })
</script>
</div>

<style>

<script>
  function renderSuggestedProduct (product_id) {
    productAttributes = allProducts['Individual Items'].filter(function(item) {if(item.id == product_id){return item}});
    
    defaultRecommendedCartPrice = 'sub_price';
    if ($('.cart-recommendation-purchase-type.active').length > 0) {
      purchaseTypeId = $('.cart-recommendation-purchase-type.active')[0].id;
      
      if (purchaseTypeId === 'cart-recommendation-one-time-purchase') {
        defaultRecommendedCartPrice = 'price';
      } else if (purchaseTypeId === 'cart-recommendation-subscription-purchase') {
        defaultRecommendedCartPrice = 'sub_price';
      }
    }
    
    return `
      <div id="product-${productAttributes[0].id}" class="recommended-product">
        <img src="${productAttributes[0].image_url}" alt="" />
        <h5 class="product-title">${productAttributes[0].title}</h5>
        <div class="product-price">
          <h5 class="discount-price">${productAttributes[0][defaultRecommendedCartPrice]}</h5>&nbsp; &nbsp; 
          <h5 class="actual-price isSubscription">${productAttributes[0].price}</h5>
        </div>
        <div onclick="addRecommendedProduct(this); return false;" class="add-recommended-product"> Add </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }
  
  function addRecommendedProduct (div) {
    window.trackingData = {
      url: window.location.href,
    }
    amplitude.getInstance().logEvent("HP: Item added to Quick Checkout", trackingData);
    
    recommendedProductId = $(div).closest('.recommended-product')[0].id.substring(8);
    
    $('.cart-recommendation-section').append( renderRecommendedProduct(recommendedProductId) );
    
    var recommendedProductIds = [];
    $(".cart-recommendation-section").find(".cart-recommendation-product").each(function(){ recommendedProductIds.push(this.id.substring(8)); });
    
    allPossibleIds = Object.values(getStartedProductsOptions);
    
    allPossibleIds = allPossibleIds.filter( ( el ) => !recommendedProductIds.includes( el ) );
    
    $('.recommended-products').empty();
    
    allPossibleIds.splice(0,2).forEach(function(item) {
      $('.recommended-products').append( renderSuggestedProduct(item) );
    });
    
    getRecommendedCartTotal();
  }
  
  $('.skincare-form-step-4-continue').click(function(evt) {
    evt.preventDefault();
    
    window.trackingData = {
      url: window.location.href,
    }
    amplitude.getInstance().logEvent("HP: Quick Checkout Pressed", trackingData);
    
    var recommendedProductIds = [];
    $(".cart-recommendation-section").find(".cart-recommendation-product").each(function(){ recommendedProductIds.push(this.id.substring(8)); });
    
    defaultCartCheckoutCurrency = 'usd';
    if (localStorage.getItem("cartCurrency") !== null) {
      defaultCartCheckoutCurrency = localStorage.getItem("cartCurrency");
    }
    if ($(".recommendCartCurrencySelect").val() !== '') {
      defaultCartCheckoutCurrency = $(".recommendCartCurrencySelect").val();
    }
    
    defaultCartCheckoutAgeBracket = '25-34';
    if ($(".skincare-form [name='age']").val() !== '') {
      defaultCartCheckoutAgeBracket = $(".skincare-form [name='age']").val();
    }
    
    defaultCartCheckoutSkinType = 'Combination';
    if ($(".skincare-form [name='skin']").val() !== '') {
      defaultCartCheckoutSkinType = $(".skincare-form [name='skin']").val();
    }
    
    defaultIntervalCount = 2;
    if ($('.cart-recommendation-purchase-type.active').length > 0) {
      purchaseTypeId = $('.cart-recommendation-purchase-type.active')[0].id;
      
      if (purchaseTypeId === 'cart-recommendation-one-time-purchase') {
        defaultIntervalCount = null;
      } else if (purchaseTypeId === 'cart-recommendation-subscription-purchase') {
        defaultIntervalCount = 2;
      }
    }
    
    
    cartCheckoutProducts = {
      'coupon' : '',
      'currency' : defaultCartCheckoutCurrency,
      'products' : []
    }
    
    for (var i = 0; i < recommendedProductIds.length; i++) {
      productAttributes = allProducts['Individual Items'].filter(function(item) {if(item.id == recommendedProductIds[i]){return item}});
      
      cartCheckoutProducts.products.push({
        'quantity' : 1,
        'interval_count' : defaultIntervalCount,
        'label_display_text' : $(".skincare-form [name='name']").val(),
        'product_option_value_ids' : getProductVariants(productAttributes[0].title, defaultCartCheckoutAgeBracket, defaultCartCheckoutSkinType)
      })
    }
    
    checkOutCart(cartCheckoutProducts);
  });
</script>

<script>
  $('button.carousel-next-button').click(function(evt){
    evt.preventDefault();
    
    var currentStep = $('.bestseller-tile.active')[0].id.substr(19);
    
    $('.bestseller-tile').removeClass('active');
    $('.bestseller-carousel-indicator').removeClass('active');
    if (currentStep < 4) {
      currentStep = parseInt(currentStep) + 1;
      $('#bestseller-product-' + currentStep).addClass('active');
      $('#bestseller-indicator-' + currentStep).addClass('active');
    } else {
      $('#bestseller-product-1').addClass('active');
      $('#bestseller-indicator-1').addClass('active');
    }
  })
  
  $('button.carousel-previous-button').click(function(evt){
    evt.preventDefault();
    
    var currentStep = $('.bestseller-tile.active')[0].id.substr(19);
    
    $('.bestseller-tile').removeClass('active');
    $('.bestseller-carousel-indicator').removeClass('active');
    if (currentStep > 1) {
      currentStep = parseInt(currentStep) - 1;
      $('#bestseller-product-' + currentStep).addClass('active');
      $('#bestseller-indicator-' + currentStep).addClass('active');
    } else {
      $('#bestseller-product-4').addClass('active');
      $('#bestseller-indicator-4').addClass('active');
    }
  })
</script>
</div>

<script>

$('.get-started-step-2-button').click(function(evt){
  evt.preventDefault();
  
  console.log('leaving step 2');
  
  if ($('.skincare-concern-option .checkbox.active').length < 3) {
    window.trackingData = {
      url: window.location.href,
    }
    amplitude.getInstance().logEvent("HP: Let's Get Started Section Opened", trackingData);
  
    $('.get-started-step-1').hide();
    $('.get-started-step-2').hide();
    $('.get-started-step-4').hide();
    $('.get-started-step-3').css('display', 'flex');
  }
});


$('.get-started-step-3-button, .get-started-step-3-skip').click(function(evt){
  evt.preventDefault();
  
  console.log('leaving step 2');
  
  if ($(".skincare-form select[name='age']").val() !== '' && $(".skincare-form select[name='skin']").val() !== '') {
    $('.unvalidated-skincare-form').hide();
    window.trackingData = {
      url: window.location.href,
    }
    amplitude.getInstance().logEvent("HP: Recommendations Section Opened", trackingData);
    
    if ($(".skincare-form input[name='email']").val() !== '') {
      _dcq.push(["identify", {
        email: $(".skincare-form input[name='email']").val(),
        first_name: $(".skincare-form input[name='name']").val(),
        tags: ["HP Duo Flow"],
        success: function(response) {
          console.log("sent to drip")
        }
      }]);
      
      _dcq.push(["identify", {
        email: $(".skincare-form input[name='email']").val(),
        first_name: $(".skincare-form input[name='name']").val(),
        tags: ["Customer"].concat($('.checkbox.active').siblings('.option-title').map(function(){return 'Concern: ' + $(this).data('item')}).toArray()),
        success: function(response) {
          console.log("sent to drip")
        }
      }]);
    }
    
    // if ($('.skincare-concern-option .checkbox.active').length == 0) {
    //   renderDefaultProduct([2,3]);
    // } else {
      recommendedProducts = [];
      $('.cart-recommendation-section').empty();
      $('.recommended-products').empty();
      
      renderDefaultProduct([2,3]);
      
      $('.skincare-concern-option .checkbox.active').parent().each(function(){
        $('.cart-recommendation-section').append( renderRecommendedProduct(getStartedProductsOptions[this.id]) );
        recommendedProducts.push(getStartedProductsOptions[this.id]);
      });
      
      allPossibleIds = Object.values(getStartedProductsOptions);
      
      allPossibleIds = allPossibleIds.filter( ( el ) => !recommendedProducts.includes( el ) );
      
      allPossibleIds.splice(0,2).forEach(function(item) {
        $('.recommended-products').append( renderSuggestedProduct(item) );
      });
      
      getRecommendedCartTotal();
    // }
    
    $('.get-started-step-1').hide();
    $('.get-started-step-2').hide();
    $('.get-started-step-3').hide();
    $('.get-started-step-4').css('display', 'flex');
  } else {
    $('.unvalidated-skincare-form').show();
  }
});

<script>
  $('.mailing-list-form input').keyup(function(){
    if (validateEmail($(this).val())) {
      $(this).siblings('.mailing-list-form button').show();
    } else {
      $(this).siblings('.mailing-list-form button').hide();
    }
  })
  
  $('.mailing-list-form button').click(function(evt) {
  	evt.preventDefault();
    
    _dcq.push(["identify", {
      email: $(this).siblings('input').val(),
      tags: ["Email List Sign Up"],
      success: function(response) {
        console.log("sent to drip");
        var success_message = "Thank you, you've been added to our email list!";
        
        $('#customModal .modal-body p').html(success_message);

      	$('#customModal').show();
        
        $('.mailing-list-form input').val('');
        $('.mailing-list-form button').hide();
      }
    }]);
  })
  
  function validateEmail(email) {
    var re = /^(([^<>()\[\]\\.,;:\s@"]+(\.[^<>()\[\]\\.,;:\s@"]+)*)|(".+"))@((\[[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\])|(([a-zA-Z\-0-9]+\.)+[a-zA-Z]{2,}))$/;
    return re.test(String(email).toLowerCase());
  }
</script>

  window["base_skin_url"] = "https://app.luminskin.com/";
  window.couponObject = '';
  
  var url_string = new URL(window.location.href);
  var url_env_param = url_string.searchParams.get("dev_environment");
  if (url_env_param == 'development'){
	window["base_skin_url"] = "https://app.dev.luminskin.com/";
  } else if (url_env_param == 'staging') {
    window["base_skin_url"] = "https://app.staging.luminskin.com/";
  }
  var url_coupon_param = url_string.searchParams.get("_referral_coupon");
  if (url_coupon_param && url_coupon_param !==null && url_coupon_param !=='') {
    doesCouponExistUrl = base_skin_url + 'api/coupons/code/' + url_coupon_param;
    $.get(doesCouponExistUrl).done(function(couponObject){
      window.couponObject = couponObject;
      // Show Modal
      if (couponObject['data'][0].free_trial === true) {
        var coupon_message = "Welcome to the Lumin Club! Your referral discount has been applied to your cart. Select a box for your free trial";
        
        $('#customModal .modal-body p').html(coupon_message);

      	$('#customModal').show();
		$('#s-de9670a1-253b-46c6-b233-6a598873c95f')[0].scrollIntoView(true);
//         $('#getStarted')[0].scrollIntoView(true);
      }
    }).fail(function(err){
      // Catch Error
      removeCouponIfExists();
//       if(err.responseJSON.status_code == 409){
//         var error_message = err.responseJSON.message;
//       } else {
        var error_message = "Your referral coupon in invalid. Please contact support@luminskin.com if you believe there was a mistake!";
      	
        $('#customModal .modal-body p').html(error_message);

      	$('#customModal').show();
//       }

      window.couponObject = false;
    })
  }

  $('.subscription-button').click(function(evt) {
    evt.preventDefault();

    //Reflect 20% discount in Cart State
    cartState = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("cartState"));

    cartState.products.forEach(function(product) {
      product.interval_count = 2
    })

    localStorage.setItem('cartState', JSON.stringify(cartState));
    // $.cookie("cartState", JSON.stringify(cartState));

    // Save Discount status
    localStorage.setItem('hasDiscount', true);

    $('.subscription-button').hide();
    $('.revert-button').show();
    $('#subscription-notice').show();

    $('#cart-item-list').empty();
    showCart();
  });

  $('.revert-button').click(function(evt) {
    evt.preventDefault();

    //Reflect 20% discount removal in Cart State
    cartState = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("cartState"));

    cartState.products.forEach(function(product) {
      product.interval_count = null
    })

    localStorage.setItem('cartState', JSON.stringify(cartState));
    // $.cookie("cartState", JSON.stringify(cartState));

    // Remove Discount status
    localStorage.removeItem('hasDiscount');

    $('.revert-button').hide();
    $('#subscription-notice').hide();
    $('.subscription-button').show();

    $('#cart-item-list').empty();
    showCart();
  });

  $('.back-arrow-icon, .close-personalize-button').click(function(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    localStorage.removeItem('selectedProduct');
    $('.personalize-overlay').css('width', '0');
    $('.cart-overlay').css('width', '0');
    $('#front-chat-container').show();
    $('form #productOptions').empty();
    
    // if ($(window).width() < 768) {
    //   $('#header').show();
    // } else {
    //   $('.header.mm-slideout').show();
    // }
    // $('iframe#launcher').show();
    $('#cart-item-list').empty();
  })
  
  $('.add-to-cart-button a').click(function(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    var attr = $(this).attr('disabled');
  
    if (!(typeof attr !== typeof undefined && attr !== false)) {
      $('.validate_error').hide();
      var labelName = '';
      var product = {};
      var cartProduct = {};
      var productPrice = 'price';
      var found = true;
      var hasDiscount = false;
      
      // Save Personalized informations
      selectedProductPersonalizeInfo = $('form#personalize-form').serializeArray();
      
      selectedProductPersonalizeInfo.forEach(function(obj, index){
        if (obj.name === "label_display_text") {
          labelName = obj.value
          selectedProductPersonalizeInfo.splice(index,1);
        }
      })
      selectedProductPersonalizeInfo.forEach(function(obj, index){
        if (obj.name === "variation_product_section") {
          productSection = obj.value
          selectedProductPersonalizeInfo.splice(index,1);
        } 
      })
      
      // Save to Local Storage and Cookie
      // localStorage.setItem('personalized_info', JSON.stringify(personalized_info));
      // $.cookie("personalized_info", JSON.stringify(personalized_info));
      selectedProduct = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("selectedProduct"));
      
      $.unique(selectedProduct.product_options).forEach(function(option){
        $.each(option, function(index, values) {
          let obj = selectedProductPersonalizeInfo.find(o => o.name == index);
          product[index] = values[obj.value];
        })
      });
      
      if ($(window).width() < 768) {
        $('.cart-overlay').css('width', '100%');
      } else if ($(window).width() < 1024) {
        $('.cart-overlay').css('width', '50%');
      } else {
        $('.cart-overlay').css('width', '30%');
      }
      $('#front-chat-container').hide();
      
      cartProduct = {
        'id' : selectedProduct.id,
        'image' : selectedProduct.image_url,
        'title' : selectedProduct.title,
        'price' : selectedProduct.price,
        'sub_price' : selectedProduct.sub_price,
        'section' : productSection,
        'quantity' : 1,
        'label_name' : labelName,
      }
      $.each(product, function(index, value) {
      	cartProduct[convertToLowerCase(index)] = value;
      })
      
      stateItem = {
        'quantity' : 1,
        'label_display_text': labelName,
        'interval_count' : null,
        'product_option_value_ids' : []
      }
      stateItem['product_option_value_ids'] = selectedProductPersonalizeInfo.map(a => a.value);
      
      cartProducts = checkAndAddProduct(cartProduct, stateItem);
      
      if (!found) {
        $('.subscription-button').show();
        
        if (localStorage.getItem("hasDiscount") !== null) {
          hasDiscount = true;
          productPrice = 'sub_price';
          // if (productSection == 'Individual Items') {
            cartState = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("cartState"));
        
            cartState.products[0].interval_count = 2;
            
            localStorage.setItem('cartState', JSON.stringify(cartState));
            // $.cookie("cartState", JSON.stringify(cartState));
          // }
          $('.subscription-button').hide();
          $('.revert-button').show();
          $('#subscription-notice').show();
        }
      } else {
        //Reflect 20% discount removal in Cart State
        cartState = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("cartState"));
        
        cartState.products.forEach(function(product) {
          product.interval_count = null;
        })
        
        localStorage.setItem('cartState', JSON.stringify(cartState));
        // $.cookie("cartState", JSON.stringify(cartState));
      }
      
      $.unique(cartProducts).forEach(function(cartProduct){
        $('#cart-item-list').append( renderCartItem(cartProduct.id, cartProduct.image, cartProduct.title, cartProduct.skin_type, cartProduct.age_bracket, subscriptionText(hasDiscount, cartProduct.size), cartProduct.quantity, cartProduct[productPrice], cartProduct.color, cartProduct.label_name) );
      });
      
      localStorage.setItem('cartProducts', JSON.stringify(cartProducts));
      // $.cookie("cartProducts", JSON.stringify(cartProducts));
      
      cartPrice = 0.00;
      
      cartProducts.forEach(function(product) {
        productAmount = product[productPrice].toString().replace(/[^0-9-.]/g, '');
        if (productAmount.startsWith('.')) {
            productAmount = productAmount.toString().substring(1);
        }
        cartPrice += parseFloat(productAmount)  * parseFloat(product.quantity);
      })
      
      // Show Cart Estimated Price
      $('#estimated-price').html( formatPrice(cartProducts[0]['price'], cartPrice) );
      
      if (defaultLanguageType !== 'en') {
        translate('en', defaultLanguageType, '#cart-item-list *');
      }
      
      if (!found) {
        $('.subscription-button').click();
      }
    } else {
      $('.validate_error').show();
    }
  })
  
  // Close Cart Modal
  $('.close-cart-overlay').click(function(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    localStorage.removeItem('selectedProduct');
    $('.personalize-overlay').css('width', '0');
    $('form #productOptions').empty();
    $('.cart-overlay').css('width', '0');
    $('#front-chat-container').show();
    $('#cart-item-list').empty();
    // if ($(window).width() < 768) {
    //   $('#header').show();
    // } else {
    //   $('.header.mm-slideout').show();
    // }
    // $('iframe#launcher').show();
    
    // $('#popular').empty();
    // $('#individual').empty();
    // $('#accessories').empty();
    products = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("products"));
    // renderLandingPageContent (products);
  })
  
  $('.cart-checkout').click(function(evt) {
    evt.preventDefault();
  
    cartState = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("cartState"));
    
    if (cartState && cartState.products.length > 0) {
      checkOutCart(cartState);
    }
  })
  
  $('.continue-to-billing a').click(function(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    
    var productTitleKey = $("input#variation_product_section").val();
    var skin_type = $("select[name='skin_type1']").val();
    var age_group = $("select[name='age_group1']").val();
    var plan_type = 'free-trial-plan';
    var plan_type_options = {
      'free-trial-plan' : 'two_month_free_trial',
      'bulk-purchase-plan' : 'bulk'
    };
    
    if (skin_type === null || age_group === null) {
      $('.validate_error').show();
      return false
    } else {
      $('.validate_error').hide();
    }
    
    $('.pricing-plan').each(function(sample){ 
    	if($(this).hasClass('active')) {
    		plan_type = $(this)[0].id;
    	} 
    })
    
    if ($(".pricing-option.active")[0]){
      // Do something if class exists
      plan_type = $('.pricing-option.active')[0].id;
    } else {
      // Do something if class does not exist
    }
    
    if ($('.purchase-button').length > 0){
      if ($('.purchase-button.active').attr("class").includes('bulk')) {
        plan_type = 'bulk-purchase-plan';
      }
    }
    
    var product_title = productTitleKey || "Classic Maintenance for Men";
    // var payment_type = window["planDetailsVariation"][plan_type_options[plan_type]]["linkName"];
    // var keys = window["planDetailsVariation"][plan_type_options[plan_type]]["keys"];
    // var size = window["planDetailsVariation"][plan_type_options[plan_type]]["size"];
    // var redirect_link = window.base_skin_url + "cart/create?skin_type=" + skin_type + "&age_group=" + age_group + "&product_title=" + product_title + "&size=" + size  + "&" + keys;
    // console.log(redirect_link);
    
    
    // window.location.href = redirect_link;
    
    // return false;
    
    var new_plan_type_options = {
      'free-trial-plan' : 'FREE_TRIAL',
      'bulk-purchase-plan' : 'BUY2GET1',
      'free_trial' : 'FREE_TRIAL',
      'bulk_purchase' : 'BUY2GET1',
    };
    
    checkoutCurrency = 'USD';
    checkoutCoupon = new_plan_type_options[plan_type];
    
    if (localStorage.getItem("skinCurrency") !== null) {
      checkoutCurrency = localStorage.getItem("skinCurrency");
    }
    
    if (localStorage.getItem("cartCurrency") !== null) {
      checkoutCurrency = localStorage.getItem("cartCurrency");
    }
    
    if (getCouponIfExists() !== '') {
      checkoutCoupon = getCouponIfExists();
    }
    
    var postDataContent = {
      "currency":checkoutCurrency,
      "coupon": checkoutCoupon,
      "products": [{
        'interval_count': 2,
        'label_display_text': $('#label_display_text').val(),
        'product_option_value_ids': getProductVariants(product_title, skin_type, age_group),
        'quantity': 1
      }]
    }
    
    if (plan_type == 'free_trial' || plan_type == 'free-trial-plan') {
      postDataContent = {
        "currency":checkoutCurrency,
        "coupon": 'FREE_TRIAL',
        "products": [{
          'interval_count': 2,
          'label_display_text': $('#label_display_text').val(),
          'product_option_value_ids': getProductVariants(product_title, skin_type, age_group),
          'quantity': 1
        }]
      }
    }
    
    if (getCouponIfExists() !== '') {
      postDataContent.coupon = getCouponIfExists();
    }
    
    if (window.couponObject) {
      if (window.couponObject['data'] && window.couponObject['data'][0].free_trial === true) {
        postDataContent.coupon = checkoutCoupon;
      }
    }
   
    if (plan_type == 'bulk_purchase' || plan_type == 'bulk-purchase-plan') {
      postDataContent = {
        "currency":checkoutCurrency,
        "coupon": 'BUY2GET1',
        "products": [{
          'label_display_text': $('#label_display_text').val(),
          'product_option_value_ids': getProductVariants(product_title, skin_type, age_group),
          'quantity': 3
        }]
      }
    }
    
    if (plan_type == 'single_purchase' || plan_type == 'single-purchase-plan') {
      postDataContent = {
        "currency":checkoutCurrency,
        "coupon": '',
        "products": [{
          'interval_count': null,
          'label_display_text': $('#label_display_text').val(),
          'product_option_value_ids': getProductVariants(product_title, skin_type, age_group),
          'quantity': 1
        }]
      }
    }
    
    if (window.couponObject) {
      if (window.couponObject['data'] && window.couponObject['data'][0].free_trial === false) {
        if (plan_type !== 'free_trial' && plan_type !== 'free-trial-plan') {
          postDataContent.coupon = checkoutCoupon;
        }
      }
    }
    
    checkOutCart(postDataContent);
  })

  if (localStorage.getItem("products") !== null) {
    storedProducts = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("products"));

    defaultCurr = 'USD';
    if (localStorage.getItem("cartCurrency") !== null) {
      defaultCurr = storedProducts.currency.toUpperCase();
    }

    // Reload API Call to check if Product is the same as local storage
    // requestResource(defaultCurr, function () {
      products = convertCurrencyToProduct(allCurrencies[defaultCurr]);
      if (JSON.stringify(products) !== JSON.stringify(storedProducts) ) {
        localStorage.removeItem('cartState'); 
        // $.removeCookie('cartState', { path: '/' });
        localStorage.removeItem('cartProducts');
        // $.removeCookie('cartProducts', { path: '/' });
        localStorage.removeItem('hasDiscount');
        localStorage.removeItem('cartCurrency');
        localStorage.removeItem('preferredOptions');
        // updateCartIconCounter();

        // Save to Local Storage and Cookie
        localStorage.setItem('products', JSON.stringify(products));
        // $.cookie("products", JSON.stringify(products), { expires : 7 });
      }
    // });

    $('#cartOverlay').remove();
  } else {
    // requestResource('USD', function () {
      products = convertCurrencyToProduct(allCurrencies['USD']);
      // Save to Local Storage and Cookie
      localStorage.setItem('products', JSON.stringify(products));
      // $.cookie("products", JSON.stringify(products), { expires : 7 });

      $('#cartOverlay').remove();
    // });
  }
  
  $('.cart-overlay').css('width', '0');
  $('.personalize-overlay').css('width', '0');
  
  $( window ).resize(function() {

      if ($(window).width() < 768) {
        $('.personalize-overlay').css('width', '100%');
      } else if ($(window).width() < 1024) {
        $('.personalize-overlay').css('width', '50%');
      } else {
        $('.personalize-overlay').css('width', '30%');
      }
    }
  });

  function convertCurrencyToProduct(currency) {
    currency['interval_count'] = currencySample['interval_count'];
    var i;
    for (i = 0; i < currency['Popular Bundles'].length; i++) { 
      currencySample['Popular Bundles'].forEach(function(product) {
        if (currency['Popular Bundles'][i].id === product.id) {
          currency['Popular Bundles'][i].title = product.title;
          currency['Popular Bundles'][i].image_url = product.image_url;
          currency['Popular Bundles'][i].thumbnail_url = product.thumbnail_url;
          currency['Popular Bundles'][i].thumbnail_url_720 = product.thumbnail_url_720;
          currency['Popular Bundles'][i].thumbnail_url_2048 = product.thumbnail_url_2048;
          currency['Popular Bundles'][i].product_options = product.product_options;
        }
      })
    }
    for (i = 0; i < currency['Individual Items'].length; i++) { 
      currencySample['Individual Items'].forEach(function(product) {
        if (currency['Individual Items'][i].id === product.id) {
          currency['Individual Items'][i].title = product.title;
          currency['Individual Items'][i].image_url = product.image_url;
          currency['Individual Items'][i].thumbnail_url = product.thumbnail_url;
          currency['Individual Items'][i].thumbnail_url_720 = product.thumbnail_url_720;
          currency['Individual Items'][i].thumbnail_url_2048 = product.thumbnail_url_2048;
          currency['Individual Items'][i].product_options = product.product_options;
        }
      })
    }
    for (i = 0; i < currency['Accessories'].length; i++) { 
      currencySample['Accessories'].forEach(function(product) {
        if (currency['Accessories'][i].id === product.id) {
          currency['Accessories'][i].title = product.title;
          currency['Accessories'][i].image_url = product.image_url;
          currency['Accessories'][i].thumbnail_url = product.thumbnail_url;
          currency['Accessories'][i].thumbnail_url_720 = product.thumbnail_url_720;
          currency['Accessories'][i].thumbnail_url_2048 = product.thumbnail_url_2048;
          currency['Accessories'][i].product_options = product.product_options;
        }
      })
    }

    return currency;
  }

  function requestResource(currency, done) {
    $.get(
          `https://app.luminskin.co/api/products/checkout?currency=${currency}`,
          // `https://app.staging.luminskin.co/api/products/checkout?currency=${currency}`,
          function(data) {
          products = data;
          done();
          // Save to Local Storage and Cookie
          localStorage.setItem('products', JSON.stringify(products));
          // $.cookie("products", JSON.stringify(products), { expires : 7 });
        }
    );
  }

  function showCart () {
    $('#cart-item-list').empty();
    cartProducts = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("cartProducts"));
    productPrice = 'price';
    found = false;
    hasDiscount = false;
    cartPrice = 0.00;
    $('#estimated-price').html( cartPrice );
    $('.subscription-button').hide();

    if (localStorage.getItem("cartProducts") !== null) {
      if (cartProducts.length > 0) {
        $('.subscription-button').show();

        if (!found) {
          $('.subscription-button').show();

          if (localStorage.getItem("hasDiscount") !== null) {
            hasDiscount = true;
            productPrice = 'sub_price';
            $('.subscription-button').hide();
            $('.revert-button').show();
            $('#subscription-notice').show();
          }
        }

        $.unique(cartProducts).forEach(function(cartProduct){
          $('#cart-item-list').append( renderCartItem(cartProduct.id, cartProduct.image, cartProduct.title, cartProduct.skin_type, cartProduct.age_bracket, subscriptionText(hasDiscount, cartProduct.size), cartProduct.quantity, cartProduct[productPrice], cartProduct.color, cartProduct.label_name) );
        });

        cartProducts.forEach(function(product) {
          productAmount = product[productPrice].toString().replace(/[^0-9-.]/g, '');
          if (productAmount.startsWith('.')) {
              productAmount = productAmount.toString().substring(1);
          }
          cartPrice += parseFloat(productAmount)  * parseFloat(product.quantity);
        })

        // Show Cart Estimated Price
        $('#estimated-price').html( formatPrice(cartProducts[0]['price'], cartPrice) );
    
        if (defaultLanguageType !== 'en') {
          translate('en', defaultLanguageType, '#cart-item-list *');
        }
      } else {
        $('#cart-item-list').html( showEmptyCart() );
      }
    } else {
      $('#cart-item-list').html( showEmptyCart() );
    }

    if ($(window).width() < 768) {
      $('.cart-overlay').css('width', '100%');
    } else if ($(window).width() < 1024) {
      $('.cart-overlay').css('width', '50%');
    } else {
      $('.cart-overlay').css('width', '30%');
    }
    $('#front-chat-container').hide();
  }

  function showEmptyCart () {
    return `<div id="empty-cart">
              <p style="color: black;text-align: center;font-size: 120%;margin-top: 20px;">
                There are no items in your cart
              </p>
            </div>`
  }
  
  function checkOutCart (cartState) {
    $.post( 
      window.base_skin_url +"api/checkout", 
      cartState
    )
    .done(function( data ) {
      checkoutUrl = data;
//       localStorage.removeItem('cartState');
//       $.removeCookie('cartState', { path: '/' });
//       localStorage.removeItem('cartProducts');
//       $.removeCookie('cartProducts', { path: '/' });
//       localStorage.removeItem('hasDiscount');
      removeCouponIfExists();
      // data["data"][0].cart.url
      if (typeof(data) === 'object'){
        checkoutUrl = data["data"][0].cart_url;
      }
      console.log("checkout callback", JSON.stringify(data));
      window.location.href = checkoutUrl + '?language=' + window.defaultLanguageType;
    });
  }

  function renderCartItem(id, imgUrl, name, age, skin, supply, quantity, price, color, labelName) {
    ageSpan = '';
    skinSpan = '';
    supplySpan = '';
    colorSpan = '';
    if (typeof age !== typeof undefined && age !== false) {
      ageSpan = '<span class="age">' + age +  '</span>';
    }
    if (typeof skin !== typeof undefined && skin !== false) {
      skinSpan = '| <span class="skin">' + skin +  '</span>';
    }
    if (typeof supply !== typeof undefined && supply !== false) {
      supplySpan = '<br><span class="supply">' + supply +  '</span>';
    }
    if (typeof color !== typeof undefined && color !== false) {
      colorSpan = '<br><span class="color">' + color +  '</span>';
    }

    pricedProduct = price.toString().replace(/[^0-9-.]/g, '');
    if (pricedProduct.startsWith('.')) {
        pricedProduct = pricedProduct.toString().substring(1);
    }

    return `<div id="${labelName + '-' + id}" class="cart-product shg-row">
              <div class="product-image">
                <img src=" ${imgUrl} " alt="Product Image">
              </div>
              <div class="product-description">
                <span onclick="removeProduct(this); return false;" class="remove-product" style="">x</span>
                <h6 style="min-height: 15px; box-sizing: border-box;"> ${name} </h6>
                <span> <b>Made For:</b> ${labelName} </span> <br>
                ${ageSpan} ${skinSpan}
                ${colorSpan}
                ${supplySpan}
                <br>
                <div class="quantity">
                  <span onclick="decreaseQuantity(this); return false;" class="counter-action decrement">-</span> <span class="counter-number counter">  ${quantity}  </span> <span onclick="increaseQuantity(this); return false;" class="counter-action increment"> + </span>
                  <div class="price" style="float:right; padding: 0 10px;letter-spacing: 1px;">
                    ${ formatPrice(price, parseFloat(pricedProduct)  * parseFloat(quantity)) }
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>`
  };

  function formatPrice(samplePrice, price) {
    var match = /[^0-9-.]/g.exec(samplePrice);
    var matchIndex = 0;
    var formattedPrice = ''
      if (match) {
        matchIndex = match.index;
      }

      if (match === null) {
        formattedPrice = parseFloat(price).toFixed(2);
      } else if (matchIndex == 0) {
        formattedPrice = match + parseFloat(price).toFixed(2);
      } else {
        formattedPrice = parseFloat(price).toFixed(2) + match;
      }

      return formattedPrice;
  }

  function removeProduct(el) {
    console.log("Remove product for", el);
    cartProducts = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("cartProducts"));
    cartState = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("cartState"));

    cartProducts.forEach(function(product, index) {
      namedID = product.label_name + '-' + product.id;
      if (namedID == $(el).closest('.cart-product').attr('id')) {
        cartProducts.splice(index,1);
        cartState['products'].splice(index,1);
      }
    })

    localStorage.setItem('cartProducts', JSON.stringify(cartProducts));
    localStorage.setItem('cartState', JSON.stringify(cartState));

    $(el).closest('.cart-product').remove();

    $('#cart-item-list').empty();
    showCart();
    updateCartIconCounter();
  }

  function decreaseQuantity(el) {
    if (parseInt($(el).siblings('.counter-number.counter').html(), 10) > 1) {
      $(el).siblings('.counter-number.counter').html(parseInt($(el).siblings('.counter-number.counter').html(), 10) - 1);
    } else {
      removeProduct(el);
      return false;
    }

    cartProducts = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("cartProducts"));
    cartState = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("cartState"));

    cartProducts.forEach(function(product, index) {
      namedID = product.label_name + '-' + product.id;
      if (namedID == $(el).closest('.cart-product').attr('id')) {
        product.quantity = parseInt($(el).siblings('.counter-number.counter').html(), 10);
        cartState['products'][index].quantity = parseInt($(el).siblings('.counter-number.counter').html(), 10);

        priceEstimated = $('#estimated-price').html().toString().replace(/[^0-9-.]/g, '');
        pricedProduct = product.price.toString().replace(/[^0-9-.]/g, '');

        if (priceEstimated.startsWith('.')) {
            priceEstimated = priceEstimated.toString().substring(1);
        }
        if (pricedProduct.startsWith('.')) {
            pricedProduct = pricedProduct.toString().substring(1);
        }

        subtotal = parseFloat(priceEstimated) - parseFloat(pricedProduct);
        $(el).siblings('.price').html(formatPrice(product.price, product.quantity * parseFloat(pricedProduct)));
        $('#estimated-price').html( formatPrice(product.price, subtotal) );
      }
    })

    localStorage.setItem('cartProducts', JSON.stringify(cartProducts));
    localStorage.setItem('cartState', JSON.stringify(cartState));

    $('#cart-item-list').empty();
    showCart();
  }

  function increaseQuantity(el) {
    if (parseInt($(el).siblings('.counter-number.counter').html(), 10) > 0) {
      $(el).siblings('.counter-number.counter').html(parseInt($(el).siblings('.counter-number.counter').html(), 10) + 1); 
    }

    cartProducts = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("cartProducts"));
    cartState = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("cartState"));

    cartProducts.forEach(function(product, index) {
      namedID = product.label_name + '-' + product.id;
      if (namedID == $(el).closest('.cart-product').attr('id')) {
        product.quantity = parseInt($(el).siblings('.counter-number.counter').html(), 10);
        cartState['products'][index].quantity = parseInt($(el).siblings('.counter-number.counter').html(), 10);

        priceEstimated = $('#estimated-price').html().toString().replace(/[^0-9-.]/g, '');
        pricedProduct = product.price.toString().replace(/[^0-9-.]/g, '');

        if (priceEstimated.startsWith('.')) {
            priceEstimated = priceEstimated.toString().substring(1);
        }
        if (pricedProduct.startsWith('.')) {
            pricedProduct = pricedProduct.toString().substring(1);
        }

        subtotal = parseFloat(priceEstimated) + parseFloat(pricedProduct);
        $(el).siblings('.price').html(formatPrice(product.price, product.quantity * parseFloat(pricedProduct)));
        $('#estimated-price').html( formatPrice(product.price, subtotal) );
      }
    })

    localStorage.setItem('cartProducts', JSON.stringify(cartProducts));
    localStorage.setItem('cartState', JSON.stringify(cartState));

    $('#cart-item-list').empty();
    showCart();
  }

  function convertToLowerCase (name) {
    return name.toLowerCase().replace(" ", "_")
  }
  
  function getCouponIfExists () {
    var coupon = '';
    url_string = new URL(window.location.href);
  	url_coupon_param = url_string.searchParams.get("_referral_coupon");
    
//     if (localStorage.getItem("checkOutCoupon") !== null) {
//       coupon = localStorage.getItem("checkOutCoupon");
//     }
    if (typeof window.optlyShippingOverride !== 'undefined' && (window.optlyShippingOverride === 'high' || window.optlyShippingOverride === 'FREE_TRIALK')) {
      // the variable is defined
      coupon = 'FREE_TRIALK';
    } else if (typeof window.optlyShippingOverride !== 'undefined' && (window.optlyShippingOverride === 'low' || window.optlyShippingOverride === 'FREE_TRIALJ')) {
      // the variable is defined
      coupon = 'FREE_TRIALJ';
    } else if (typeof window.optlyShippingOverride !== 'undefined' && window.optlyShippingOverride === 'FREE_TRIALL') {
      // the variable is defined
      coupon = 'FREE_TRIALL';
    } else if (typeof window.optlyShippingOverride !== 'undefined' && window.optlyShippingOverride === 'FREE_TRIALM') {
      // the variable is defined
      coupon = 'FREE_TRIALM';
    } else if (typeof window.optlyShippingOverride !== 'undefined' && window.optlyShippingOverride === 'FREE_TRIALI') {
      // the variable is defined
      coupon = 'FREE_TRIALI';
    }
    
    if (url_coupon_param && url_coupon_param !==null && url_coupon_param !=='' && window.couponObject !== false) {
      coupon = url_coupon_param;
//       localStorage.setItem('checkOutCoupon', coupon);
    }
    
    return coupon;
  }
  
  function removeCouponIfExists () {
    if (localStorage.getItem("checkOutCoupon") !== null) {
      localStorage.removeItem('checkOutCoupon');
    }
    
    if (localStorage.getItem("cartState") !== null) {
      cartState = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("cartState"));
      cartState.coupon = '';
      localStorage.setItem('cartState', JSON.stringify(cartState));
    }
  }

  function checkAndAddProduct(product, item) {
    cartProducts = [];
    cartState = {
      'currency' : 'usd',
      'products' : []
    }

    if (localStorage.getItem("cartProducts") !== null) {
      cartProducts = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("cartProducts"));
      cartState = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("cartState"));
      cartProducts.forEach(function(cartProduct, index){
        if (cartProduct.title === product.title && cartProduct.label_name === product.label_name) {
          item['quantity'] = cartState['products'][index].quantity;
          product['quantity'] = cartProducts[index].quantity;

          cartState['products'].splice(index,1);
          cartProducts.splice(index,1);
        } 
      })
    } 
    cartState['products'].unshift(item);
    cartProducts.unshift(product);

    // Update the Cart State
    localStorage.setItem('cartState', JSON.stringify(cartState));
    // $.cookie("cartState", JSON.stringify(cartState));

    // Update the Cart Products
    localStorage.setItem('cartProducts', JSON.stringify(cartProducts));
    // $.cookie("cartProducts", JSON.stringify(cartProducts));

    updateCartIconCounter();

    return cartProducts;
  }
  
  function getProductVariants (title, skin_type, age_group, size='Two Month') {
    var productVariants = [];
    storedProducts = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("products"));
    
    $.unique(storedProducts['Individual Items']).forEach(function(product) {
      if (product.title == title ) {
        $.unique(product['product_options']).forEach(function(option){
          $.each(option, function(index, values) {
            if (index = 'Skin Type' && getKeyByValue(values, skin_type)) {
              productVariants.push(getKeyByValue(values, skin_type));
            }
            if (index = 'Age Bracket' && getKeyByValue(values, age_group)) {
              productVariants.push(getKeyByValue(values, age_group));
            }
            if (index = 'Size' && getKeyByValue(values, size)) {
              productVariants.push(getKeyByValue(values, size));
            }
          })
        });
      }
    });
    
    $.unique(storedProducts['Popular Bundles']).forEach(function(product) {
      if (product.title == title ) {
        $.unique(product['product_options']).forEach(function(option){
          $.each(option, function(index, values) {
            if (index = 'Skin Type' && getKeyByValue(values, skin_type)) {
              productVariants.push(getKeyByValue(values, skin_type));
            }
            if (index = 'Age Bracket' && getKeyByValue(values, age_group)) {
              productVariants.push(getKeyByValue(values, age_group));
            }
            if (index = 'Size' && getKeyByValue(values, size)) {
              productVariants.push(getKeyByValue(values, size));
            }
          })
        });
      }
    });
    
    return productVariants;
  }

  function getKeyByValue(object, value) {
    return Object.keys(object).find(key => object[key] === value);
  }

  function renderOverlay () {
    return `
            <div id="cartOverlay" class="overlay">
              <img alt="Loader" src="https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/3/3a/Gray_circles_rotate.gif" decoding="async" width="200" height="200">
            </div>
          `
  }
  
  function validateForm (form, button) {
    formData = $(form).serializeArray();
    
    notValidated = formData.some(function(data){ return data.value === '' });
    
    if (notValidated) {
      $(button).attr("disabled", "disabled");
    } else {
      $(button).removeAttr('disabled');
    }
  }
  
  function personalizeProduct (id, section) {
    if(window["_tfa"]){
      _tfa.push({notify: 'event', name: 'Add_To_Cart'});
    }
    $('form #productOptions').empty();
    $('#cart-item-list').empty();
    $('#variation_product_section').val(section);
    
    if (id == 0) {
      $('.add-to-cart-button, .close-personalize-button, .personalize-image-template, .personalize-header-text, .bulk-purchase-checkout-only').hide();
      $('.continue-to-billing, .subscription-checkout-only').show();
      
      var plan_type = 'free-trial-plan';
      var plan_type_options = {
        'free-trial-plan' : 'two_month_free_trial',
        'bulk-purchase-plan' : 'bulk',
        'single-purchase-plan' : 'single',
        'free_trial' : 'two_month_free_trial',
        'bulk_purchase' : 'bulk',
        'single_purchase' : 'single',
      };
      
      $('.pricing-plan').each(function(sample){ 
      	if($(this).hasClass('active')) {
      		plan_type = $(this)[0].id;
      	} 
      })
      
      if ($(".pricing-option.active")[0]){
        // Do something if class exists
        plan_type = $('.pricing-option.active')[0].id;
      } else {
        // Do something if class does not exist
      }
      
      if (plan_type_options[plan_type] == 'bulk' || plan_type_options[plan_type] == 'single') {
        $('.free-trial-checkout-only').hide();
        $('.personalize-header-text').show();
        $('.bulk-purchase-checkout-only').show();
      }
      
      if ($('.purchase-button').length > 0){
        if ($('.purchase-button.active').attr("class").includes('bulk')) {
          $('.free-trial-checkout-only').hide();
          $('.personalize-header-text').show();
          $('.bulk-purchase-checkout-only').show();
        }
      }
      
      $(".subscription-plan-type").text(planDetailsVariation[plan_type_options[plan_type]]["price"]);
      $(".insertSubscriptionPriceVariation").text(planDetailsVariation[plan_type_options[plan_type]]["renewal_price"]);
      $(".shippingInsertCostVariation").text(planDetailsVariation[plan_type_options[plan_type]]["shipping"]);
      if ($(".everything-section .subscription-plan-type")[0]) {
        if(planDetailsVariation[plan_type_options[plan_type]]["price"] == 'FREE TRIAL') {
          $(".everything-section .subscription-plan-type").html( renderFreeTrialWithShipping() );
          console.log("RENDER FTWSHIP 1");
//           if (defaultLanguageType !== defaultFromLanguageType) {
//               translate(defaultFromLanguageType, defaultLanguageType, '.everything-section *');
//           }
        }
      }
      // $('.personalize-image-template img').attr('src', '');
      $('form #productOptions').append( renderDefaultDropDownMenu() );
      
      updateTotalCostVariationPrice();
    } else {
      $('.add-to-cart-button, .close-personalize-button, .personalize-image-template, .personalize-header-text').show();
      $('.continue-to-billing, .subscription-checkout-only, .bulk-purchase-checkout-only').hide();
      storedProducts = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("products"));
      labelName = getSelectedValue('label_display_text');
      $('#label_display_text').val(labelName);
      
      $.unique(storedProducts[section]).forEach(function(product) {
        if (product.id == id ) {
          $('.personalize-image-template img').attr('src', product.image_url);
          localStorage.setItem('selectedProduct', JSON.stringify(product));
          $.unique(product['product_options']).forEach(function(option){
            $.each(option, function(index, values) {
              $('form #productOptions').append( renderVariationDropDownMenu(index, values) );
            })
          });
        }
      });
      
      $("form#personalize-form input").change(function() {
        validateForm ('form#personalize-form', '.add-to-cart-button a');
      });
      
      $("form#personalize-form select").change(function() {
        validateForm ('form#personalize-form', '.add-to-cart-button a');
      });
      
      validateForm ('form#personalize-form', '.add-to-cart-button a');
      
      var sizeSelect = document.getElementById('size');
      if (sizeSelect !== null) {
        sizeSelect.selectedIndex = sizeSelect.options.length-1;
      }
      
      $("select#size option:last").attr("selected", "selected");
      $("select#size").hide();
      
      $("form#personalize-form .label_display_text").focus();
    }
    
    if ($(window).width() < 768) {
      $('.personalize-overlay').css('width', '100%');
    } else if ($(window).width() < 1024) {
      $('.personalize-overlay').css('width', '50%');
    } else {
      $('.personalize-overlay').css('width', '30%');
    }
    $('.cart-overlay').css('width', '0');
    $('#front-chat-container').hide();
    
    if (defaultLanguageType !== 'en') {
      translate('en', defaultLanguageType, '#productOptions *, .subscription-plan-type, .subscription-plan-type *');
    }
  }
  
  function getSelectedValue (index, name='') {
    var value = '';
    var preferredOptions = {};
    
    if (localStorage.getItem("preferredOptions") !== null) {
      preferredOptions = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("preferredOptions"));
    }
    
    if (preferredOptions.hasOwnProperty(index)) {
      if(index == 'label_display_text') {
        return preferredOptions[index];
      }
      
      if(preferredOptions[index] == name) {
        value = 'selected';
      }
    }
    
    return value;
  }

        </select>
      </p>
  
      <p class="line-item-property__field">
        <select required="" class="required" name="age_group1">
          <option disabled="" selected="" value="">Choose Age Group</option>
          <option value="13-24">13 - 24</option>
          <option value="25-34">25 - 34</option>
          <option value="35-45">35 - 45</option>
          <option value="46-55">46 - 55</option>
          <option value="56+">56+</option>
        </select>
      </p>
    `;
  }
  
  // Template to render dropdown select menu
  function renderVariationDropDownMenu(name, options) {
    var preOptions = `<p class="line-item-property__field">
                        <select id="${convertToLowerCase(name)}" required class="required" name="${name}">
                          `;
    var postOptions = `</select>
                        </p>`;
    var optionsDropdown = '';
    
    $.each(options, function(index, value){
    		optionsDropdown += `<option value="${index}">${product_options[convertToLowerCase(name)][value]}</option>`;
    })
    
    return preOptions + optionsDropdown + postOptions;
  }

  function subscriptionText (subscribed, supply) {
    var text = 'One time purchase of Two Month supply';
    
    if (subscribed) {
      text = 'Two Month supply shipped every 2 months <br> Cancel or change frequency anytime';
    }
    
    if (typeof supply !== typeof undefined && supply !== false) {
      text = 'One time purchase of ' + supply + ' supply';
      
      if (subscribed) {
        text = supply + ' supply shipped every 2 months <br> Cancel or change frequency anytime'
      }
    }
    
    return text;
  }
    
  function getPricesVariation(input){
    var currency = (input && input["currency"]) || (window.customData && customData["currency"]) || "USD";
    
    if (localStorage.getItem("cartCurrency") !== null) {
      currency = localStorage.getItem("cartCurrency");
    }
    
    if (currency == "KWD" || currency == "JOD" || currency == "OMR" || currency == "VEF" || currency == "CYP" || currency == "CYP" || currency == "LTL" || currency == "SVC" || currency == "CYP" || currency == "LTL" || currency == "SVC" || currency == "BHD" || currency == "ECS") {
      currency = "USD";
    }
    
    if(!__currencies.includes(currency.toUpperCase())) {
    	currency = "USD";
    }
    
    if (currency == undefined) {
      currency = "USD";
    }
    
    if(window.customData && customData["location"] && customData["location"]["country"] && customData["location"]["country"]["iso_code"]){
      var country = customData["location"]["country"]["iso_code"]
    } else {
      var country = "US";
    }
    
    
    var url = window.base_skin_url + "api/checkout/data?currency=" + 
            currency +  
            "&country=" + country +  
            "&language=en";
    console.log("get pricing url",url);
    
    var shippingCurrency = localStorage.getItem('cartCurrency') || localStorage.getItem('skinCurrency') || 'USD';
    
    localStorage.setItem("skinCurrency", currency)
    
    $.get(url).then(function(pricingData) { 
      console.log("pricingData", pricingData);
      var activeProducts = { // No Free Trial
        // two_month_subscription: ["Two Month", "subscription"], 
        two_month_free_trial: ["Two Month", "free_trial"], 
        single: ["Two Month","one_time"],
        bulk: ["Two Month", "bulk_3"]
      }
      
      for(var product in activeProducts){
         var size = activeProducts[product][0];
         var planType = activeProducts[product][1];
         
         if(planType == "free_trial"){
           window["planDetailsVariation"][product]["price"] = "FREE TRIAL";
           window["planDetailsVariation"][product]["renewal_price"] = pricingData["products"][0][size][planType]["renewal_price"]; 
         } else {
           window["planDetailsVariation"][product]["price"] = pricingData["products"][0][size][planType]["price"];
         }
         
         if(window["planDetailsVariation"][product]["dayPrice"]){
           window["planDetailsVariation"][product]["dayPrice"] = pricingData["products"][0][activeProducts[product][0]][activeProducts[product][1]]["per_day_price"];  
         } 
         window["planDetailsVariation"][product]["size"] = activeProducts[product][0];
         window["planDetailsVariation"][product]["keys"] = pricingData["products"][0][activeProducts[product][0]][activeProducts[product][1]]["keys"];
         window["planDetailsVariation"][product]["is_free_shipping"] = pricingData["products"][0][activeProducts[product][0]][activeProducts[product][1]]["free_shipping"];
         window["planDetailsVariation"][product]["shipping"] = pricingData["products"][0][activeProducts[product][0]][activeProducts[product][1]]["shipping"];
      }
      
      var plan_type = 'free_trial';
      var plan_type_options = {
        'free_trial' : 'two_month_free_trial',
        'bulk_purchase' : 'bulk',
        'single_purchase' : 'single',
        'free-trial-plan' : 'two_month_free_trial',
      	'bulk-purchase-plan' : 'bulk'
      };
    
      $('.pricing-plan').each(function(sample){ 
      	if($(this).hasClass('active')) {
      		plan_type = $(this)[0].id;
      	} 
      })
      
      if ($(".pricing-option.active")[0]){
        // Do something if class exists
        plan_type = $('.pricing-option.active')[0].id;
      } else {
        // Do something if class does not exist
      }
      
      $(".subscription-plan-type").text(planDetailsVariation[plan_type_options[plan_type]]["price"]);
      $(".insertSubscriptionPriceVariation").text(planDetailsVariation[plan_type_options[plan_type]]["renewal_price"]);
      $(".shippingInsertCostVariation").text(planDetailsVariation[plan_type_options[plan_type]]["shipping"]);
      
      $(".freeTrialPurchasePrice").text(formatPrice(planDetailsVariation["two_month_free_trial"]["shipping"], 0));
      $(".singlePurchasePrice").text(planDetailsVariation["single"]["price"]);
      $(".bulkPurchasePrice").text(planDetailsVariation["bulk"]["price"]);
    
      if ($(".everything-section .subscription-plan-type")[0]) {
        if(planDetailsVariation[plan_type_options[plan_type]]["price"] == 'FREE TRIAL') {
          $(".everything-section .subscription-plan-type").html( renderFreeTrialWithShipping() );
          console.log("RENDER FTWSHIP 2");
          if (defaultLanguageType !== defaultFromLanguageType) {
              translate(defaultFromLanguageType, defaultLanguageType);
          }
        }
      }
      
      if ($(".gift-plan-section .subscription-plan-type")[0]) {
        if(planDetailsVariation[plan_type_options[plan_type]]["price"] !== 'FREE TRIAL') {
          $(".gift-plan-section .subscription-plan-type").html( renderGiftItemPricing() );
        }
      }
      
      updateTotalCostVariationPrice();
    }).then(function(){ 
      if (window.cartCoupon) {
        couponDiscountUrl = base_skin_url + 'api/coupons/code/' + window.cartCoupon + '/' + shippingCurrency;
        $.get(couponDiscountUrl).done(function(couponObject){
          if (couponObject['data'][0].free_trial === true && couponObject['data'][0].coupon_type == 'override') {
//             planDetailsVariation['two_month_free_trial']["shipping"] = '$' + parseFloat(couponObject['data'][0].override_amount/100).toFixed(2);
            planDetailsVariation['two_month_free_trial']["shipping"] = couponObject['data'][0].formatted_override_amount;
            $(".shippingInsertCostVariation").text(planDetailsVariation['two_month_free_trial']["shipping"]);

            updateTotalCostVariationPrice();
          }
        })
//         .fail(function(err){
//           // Catch Error
//           if (typeof window.optlyShippingOverride !== 'undefined' && window.optlyShippingOverride === 'high') {
//               // the variable is defined
//               planDetailsVariation['two_month_free_trial']["shipping"] = '$11.95';
//               $(".shippingInsertCostVariation").text(planDetailsVariation['two_month_free_trial']["shipping"]);

//               updateTotalCostVariationPrice();
//             } else if (typeof window.optlyShippingOverride !== 'undefined' && window.optlyShippingOverride === 'low') {
//               // the variable is defined
//               planDetailsVariation['two_month_free_trial']["shipping"] = '$3.95';
//               $(".shippingInsertCostVariation").text(planDetailsVariation['two_month_free_trial']["shipping"]);

//               updateTotalCostVariationPrice();
//             }
//         })
      } 
//       else {
//         if (typeof window.optlyShippingOverride !== 'undefined' && window.optlyShippingOverride === 'high') {
//           // the variable is defined
//           planDetailsVariation['two_month_free_trial']["shipping"] = '$11.95';
//           $(".shippingInsertCostVariation").text(planDetailsVariation['two_month_free_trial']["shipping"]);

//           updateTotalCostVariationPrice();
//         } else if (typeof window.optlyShippingOverride !== 'undefined' && window.optlyShippingOverride === 'low') {
//           // the variable is defined
//           planDetailsVariation['two_month_free_trial']["shipping"] = '$3.95';
//           $(".shippingInsertCostVariation").text(planDetailsVariation['two_month_free_trial']["shipping"]);

//           updateTotalCostVariationPrice();
//         }
//       }
    });
  }
  
  function updateTotalCostVariationPrice () {
    var planPrice = $('.subscription-price-subtotal .subscription-plan-type').text().toString().replace(/[^0-9-.]/g, '');
    if (planPrice.startsWith('.')) {
        planPrice = planPrice.toString().substring(1);
    } else if (planPrice == '') {
      planPrice = 0.00;
    }
    var shippingPrice = $('.subscription-price-subtotal .shippingInsertCostVariation').text().toString().replace(/[^0-9-.]/g, '');
    if (shippingPrice.startsWith('.')) {
        shippingPrice = shippingPrice.toString().substring(1);
    } else if (shippingPrice == '') {
      shippingPrice = 0.00;
    }
    
    var totalCostPrice = formatPrice($('.subscription-price-subtotal .shippingInsertCostVariation').text(), parseFloat(planPrice)  + parseFloat(shippingPrice));
    
    $('.totalCostVariation').html(totalCostPrice);
  }
  
  function renderGiftItemPricing(){
    return '<span style="color: #D8D8D8;text-decoration: line-through;">$180</span>&nbsp;<span >$120</span>&nbsp;<span style="color: #D8D8D8;font-size: 1.2rem;font-weight: 400;">(Includes 2 months FREE)</span>'
  }
  
  function renderFreeTrialWithShipping () {
    return 'FREE TRIAL <br> <span style="font-size: 10px; font-weight: 100;"> + shipping </span>'
  }
  
  window.setTimeout(function(){
   $(".personalize-overlay").show(); 
   $(".cart-overlay").show(); 
  },3000);
</script>

<script> 
              var __currencies = ["AED", "AFN", "ALL", "AMD", "ANG", "AOA", "ARS", "AUD", "AWG", "AZN", "BAM", "BBD", "BDT", "BGN", "BIF", "BMD", "BND", "BOB", "BRL", "BSD", "BWP", "BZD", "CAD", "CDF", "CHF", "CLP", "CNY", "COP", "CRC", "CVE", "CZK", "DJF", "DKK", "DOP", "DZD", "EGP", "ETB", "EUR", "FJD", "FKP", "GBP", "GEL", "GIP", "GMD", "GNF", "GTQ", "GYD", "HKD", "HNL", "HRK", "HTG", "HUF", "IDR", "ILS", "INR", "ISK", "JMD", "JPY", "KES", "KGS", "KHR", "KMF", "KRW", "KYD", "KZT", "LAK", "LBP", "LKR", "LRD", "LSL", "MAD", "MDL", "MGA", "MKD", "MMK", "MNT", "MOP", "MRO", "MUR", "MVR", "MWK", "MXN", "MYR", "MZN", "NAD", "NGN", "NIO", "NOK", "NPR", "NZD", "PAB", "PEN", "PGK", "PHP", "PKR", "PLN", "PYG", "QAR", "RON", "RSD", "RUB", "RWF", "SAR", "SBD", "SCR", "SEK", "SGD", "SHP", "SLL", "SOS", "SRD", "STD", "SVC", "SZL", "THB", "TJS", "TOP", "TRY", "TTD", "TWD", "TZS", "UAH", "UGX", "UYU", "UZS", "VND", "VUV", "WST", "XAF", "XCD", "XOF", "XPF", "YER", "ZAR", "ZMW"]

//               $('.cart-overlay').appendTo('body');

              $.unique(__currencies).forEach(function(currency){
                if(currency == "KWD" || currency == "JOD" || currency == "OMR" || currency == "VEF" || currency == "CYP" || currency == "CYP" || currency == "LTL" || currency == "SVC" || currency == "CYP" || currency == "LTL" || currency == "SVC" || currency == "BHD" || currency == "ECS"){
                  // do nothing
                } else{
                  $(".currencySelect").append(`<option value="${currency}"> ${currency} </option>`);
                  $(".personalizeCurrencySelectMenu").append(`<option value="${currency}"> ${currency} </option>`);
                  $(".recommendCartCurrencySelect").append(`<option value="${currency}"> ${currency} </option>`);
                }
              });

              if (localStorage.getItem("products") !== null) {
                allProducts = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("products"));
                $(".currencySelect").val(allProducts.currency.toUpperCase());
                $(".personalizeCurrencySelectMenu").val(allProducts.currency.toUpperCase());
              }

              if (localStorage.getItem("cartCurrency") !== null) {
                homeCartCurrency = localStorage.getItem("cartCurrency");
                $(".personalizeCurrencySelectMenu").val(homeCartCurrency.toUpperCase());
              }

              $("body").delegate(".currencySelect", "change", function(ev){
                $('body').append( renderOverlay() );
                var curr = $(".currencySelect").val();
                $(".personalizeCurrencySelectMenu").val(curr);
                // Save to Local Storage and Cookie
                localStorage.setItem('cartCurrency', curr);
                // $.cookie("cartCurrency", curr, { expires : 7 });

                // requestResource(curr, function () {
                  products = convertCurrencyToProduct(allCurrencies[curr]);
                  // Remove 20% discount
                  cartProducts = [];

                  if (localStorage.getItem("cartProducts") !== null) {
                    cartProducts = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("cartProducts"));
                    cartState = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("cartState"));

                    cartState['currency'] = curr.toLowerCase();

                    cartPrice = 0.00;
                  }

                  if (cartProducts.length > 0) {
                    $.unique(cartProducts).forEach(function(cartProduct){
                      // Find product in products
                      products["Popular Bundles"].forEach(function(product) {
                        if (product.id == cartProduct.id) {
                          cartProduct.price = product.price;
                          cartProduct.sub_price = product.sub_price;
                        }
                      })
                      products["Individual Items"].forEach(function(product) {
                        if (product.id == cartProduct.id) {
                          cartProduct.price = product.price;
                          cartProduct.sub_price = product.sub_price;
                        }
                      })
                      products["Accessories"].forEach(function(product) {
                        if (product.id == cartProduct.id) {
                          cartProduct.price = product.price;
                          cartProduct.sub_price = product.sub_price;
                        }
                      })
                    });

                    localStorage.setItem('cartProducts', JSON.stringify(cartProducts));
                    localStorage.setItem('cartState', JSON.stringify(cartState));
                  } else {
                    delete cartProducts;
                  }

                  // Reload Cart
                  $('#cart-item-list').empty();
                  showCart();

                  $('#cartOverlay').remove();
                // });

                // Save to Local Storage and Cookie
                localStorage.setItem('products', JSON.stringify(products));
                // $.cookie("products", JSON.stringify(products), { expires : 7 });
              })

              $("body").delegate(".personalizeCurrencySelectMenu", "change", function(ev){
                var curr = $(".personalizeCurrencySelectMenu").val();
                $(".currencySelectMenu").val(curr);
                // Save to Local Storage and Cookie
                localStorage.setItem('cartCurrency', curr);
                products = convertCurrencyToProduct(allCurrencies[curr]);

                getPricesVariation({currency: curr});
                localStorage.setItem('products', JSON.stringify(products));
              })
            </script>

// Get the modal
var modal = document.getElementById("rebrandModal");

// Get the button that opens the modal
// var btn = document.getElementById("myBtn");

// Get the <span> element that closes the modal
var span = document.getElementsByClassName("close-rebrand-modal")[0];
var shopNow = document.getElementsByClassName("shop-now-section")[0];

// // When the user clicks the button, open the modal 
// btn.onclick = function() {
//   modal.style.display = "block";
// }

// Set the modal to open by default
// modal.style.display = "block";
var url_string = new URL(window.location.href);
var url_env_param = url_string.searchParams.get("referring_url");
if (url_env_param == 'menskin'){
  modal.style.display = "block";
}


// When the user clicks on <span> (x), close the modal
span.onclick = function() {
  modal.style.display = "none";
}

// When the user clicks on <span> (x), close the modal
shopNow.onclick = function() {
  modal.style.display = "none";
}

// When the user clicks anywhere outside of the modal, close it
window.onclick = function(event) {
  if (event.target == modal) {
    modal.style.display = "none";
  }
}
</script></div>
